- **levenshtein-approximate-string-matching**: Use when noisy sequencing data must be matched against a barcode/sequence whitelist allowing up to e edits (default e=2), and you need minimum-edit-distance matches rather than exact string equality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/levenshtein-approximate-string-matching))
- **levenshtein-barcode-matching**: Use when demultiplexing long-read (FASTQ/FASTA) data against a known barcode whitelist and you need edit-distance-tolerant barcode matching with a unique--match assignment rule. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/levenshtein-barcode-matching))
- **levenshtein-distance-insertion-sequence-comparison**: Use when merging multi-sample insertion callsets where callers report coordinates only and distinct events at near-identical coordinates must be preserved by comparing their inserted sequences rather than coordinates alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/levenshtein-distance-insertion-sequence-comparison))
- **levenshtein-distance-read-assignment**: Use when demultiplexing FASTQ/FASTA reads to a known list of cellular barcodes from noisy single-cell long-read data, where sequencing errors mean exact barcode matching fails and imperfect (edit-distance-tolerant) matching is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/levenshtein-distance-read-assignment))
- **levenshtein-fuzzy-name-matching**: Use when a metabolomics feature table has free-text or non-standard metabolite column names that must be converted to standard HMDB IDs before pathway mapping, and exact/synonym lookups leave names unmapped. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/levenshtein-fuzzy-name-matching))
- **levenshtein-qgram-shape-hashing**: Use when mapping high-error long reads (e.g. Oxford Nanopore MinION FASTA/FASTQ) to a large reference genome and you need to reproduce or evaluate how a coarse-alignment stage reduces the candidate search space before fine alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/levenshtein-qgram-shape-hashing))
